Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on nanotechnology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 507. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 165 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_507(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 507. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^165(S, F) is trivial. QED.
